| set | /set/ | v. 放置(位于, 以…为背景, 摆餐具); 使处于(使开始, 布置任务, 规定); 调整(设定闹钟, 设置); 凝固(做发型, 接骨, 愈合); 落下<br><br>n. 一套(一伙, 一盘, 集合); 姿态(神态, 怨恨, 流向); 电视机; 布景(片场); 卷发定型<br><br>adj. 预定的(固定不变的, 僵硬的, 套餐的); 准备好的(坚决反对的, 决心…的) | **set(放)**<br><br>把东西安放在某处 | v. put, lay, or stand (something) in a specified place or position<br>&emsp;• be situated or fixed in a specified place or position<br>&emsp;• represent (a story, play, film, or scene) as happening at a specified time or in a specified place<br>&emsp;• prepare (a table) for a meal by placing cutlery, crockery, etc. on it in their proper places<br>put or bring into a specified state<br>&emsp;• cause (someone or something) to start doing something<br>&emsp;• give someone (a task or test) to do<br>&emsp;• fix (a price, value, or limit) on something<br>adjust (a clock or watch), typically to show the right time<br>&emsp;• adjust (an alarm clock) to sound at the required time<br>&emsp;• adjust (a device) so that it performs a particular operation<br>harden into a solid or semi-solid state<br>&emsp;• arrange (the hair) while damp so that it dries in the required style<br>&emsp;• put parts of (a broken or dislocated bone or limb) into the correct position for healing<br>&emsp;• (of a bone) be restored to its normal condition by knitting together again after being broken<br>(of the sun, moon, or another celestial body) appear to move towards and below the earth's horizon as the earth rotates<br>n. a group or collection of things that belong together or resemble one another or are usually found together<br>&emsp;• a group of people with common interests or occupations or of similar social status<br>&emsp;• (in tennis, darts, and other games) a group of games counting as a unit towards a match<br>&emsp;• a collection of distinct entities regarded as a unit, being either individually specified or (more usually) satisfying specified conditions<br>the way in which something is set, disposed, or positioned<br>&emsp;• the posture or attitude of a part of the body, typically in relation to the impression this gives of a person's feelings or intentions<br>&emsp;• a grudge<br>&emsp;• the flow of a current or tide in a particular direction<br>a radio or television receiver<br>a collection of scenery, stage furniture, and other articles used for a particular scene in a play or film<br>&emsp;• the place or area in which filming is taking place or a play is performed<br>an arrangement of the hair when damp so that it dries in the required style<br>adj. fixed or arranged in advance<br>&emsp;• (of a view or habit) unlikely to change<br>&emsp;• (of a person's expression) held for an unnaturally long time without changing, typically as a reflection of determination<br>&emsp;• (of a meal or menu in a restaurant) offered at a fixed price with a limited choice of dishes<br>ready, prepared, or likely to do something<br>&emsp;• firmly opposed to<br>&emsp;• determined to do (something) | **set** a new world record | v. put, be situated, fix, adorn, assign, apply, decide on, establish<br>n. group, collection, clique, kit, apparatus, canteen, service, class<br>adj. fixed, stock, ready, determined to, opposed to, established, hard and fast, determined | 0 |

| half | /hɑːf/ | n. 一半(半场, 半品脱, 半票)<br><br>det. 一半(大约一半)<br><br>adv. 一半地(部分地) | 把整体平分后的一份 | n. either of two equal or corresponding parts into which something is or can be divided<br>&emsp;• either of two equal periods of time into which a sports game or a performance is divided<br>&emsp;• half a pint of beer or a similar drink<br>&emsp;• a half-price fare or ticket, especially for a child<br>det. an amount equal to a half<br>&emsp;• an amount thought of as roughly a half<br>adv. to the extent of half<br>&emsp;• to a certain extent; partly | **half** of the apple | n. fifty per cent of, bisection of<br>adj. halved, divided in two, part, partial, bisected, in two equal parts/portions<br>adv. partially, to a certain extent/degree, partly, incompletely, inadequately, insufficiently, slightly, barely | 0 |
| term | /tɜːm/ | n. 术语(措辞, 词项); 期限(足月, 定期租赁); 学期; 条件(付款条件, 停战条件); 项<br><br>v. 称为 | **term(界限, 期限)**<br><br>学期中划分的一段时间 | n. a word or phrase used to describe a thing or to express a concept, especially in a particular kind of language or branch of study<br>&emsp;• language used on a particular occasion; a way of expressing oneself<br>&emsp;• a word or words that may be the subject or predicate of a proposition<br>a fixed or limited period for which something, for example office, imprisonment, or investment, lasts or is intended to last<br>&emsp;• the completion of a normal length of pregnancy<br>&emsp;• a tenancy of a fixed period<br>each of the periods in the year, alternating with holiday or vacation, during which instruction is given in a school, college, or university, or during which a law court holds sessions<br>conditions under which an action may be undertaken or agreement reached; stipulated or agreed requirements<br>&emsp;• conditions with regard to payment for something; stated charges<br>&emsp;• agreed conditions under which a war or other dispute is brought to an end<br>each of the quantities in a ratio, series, or mathematical expression<br>v. give a descriptive name to; call by a specified term | in the long **term** | n. word, language, conditions, rates, period, session, expression, phrase<br>v. call, name, entitle, title, style, designate, describe as, dub | 0 |

| hold | /həʊld/ | v. 拿着(保持姿势, 拥抱, 承受得住); 扣留(守住, 吸引住, 维持在); 保持完好(持续不变, 依然有效, 站得住脚); 拥有(担任, 持有观点, 裁定); 举行<br><br>n. 抓握(擒拿法, 攀岩支点); 控制力; 货舱 | 用手握紧、把东西抓住 | v. grasp, carry, or support with one's hands<br>&emsp;• keep or sustain in a specified position<br>&emsp;• embrace (someone)<br>&emsp;• be able to bear (the weight of a person or thing)<br>keep or detain (someone)<br>&emsp;• keep possession of (something), typically in the face of a challenge or attack<br>&emsp;• keep (someone's interest or attention)<br>&emsp;• stay or cause to stay at a certain value or level<br>remain secure, intact, or in position without breaking or giving way<br>&emsp;• (of a favourable condition or situation) continue without changing<br>&emsp;• be or remain valid or available<br>&emsp;• (of an argument or theory) be logical, consistent, or convincing<br>have in one's possession<br>&emsp;• have or occupy (a job or position)<br>&emsp;• have (a belief or opinion)<br>&emsp;• (of a judge or court) rule; decide<br>arrange and take part in (a meeting or conversation)<br>n. an act or manner of grasping something; a grip<br>&emsp;• a particular way of grasping or restraining someone, especially an opponent in wrestling or judo<br>&emsp;• a place where one can grip with one's hands or feet while climbing<br>power or control<br>a large compartment or space in the lower part of a ship or aircraft in which cargo is stowed | **held** the rope tightly | v. clasp, carry, embrace, possess, support, detain, maintain, have a capacity of<br>n. grip, purchase, influence, grasp, clasp, clutch, foothold, footing | 0 |
| turn | /tɜːn/ | v. 转动(翻跟头, 扭伤); 翻转(转身, 对准, 转向); 变得(使变成, 使处于, 年满); 策反; 赚取<br><br>n. 转动(弯道, 一圈); 转向(转折, 交替之际, 岔路口); 轮流机会(表演节目, 表演者); 兜一圈; 惊吓(不适) | 绕着轴转动 | v. move in a circular direction wholly or partly round an axis or point<br>&emsp;• perform (a somersault or cartwheel)<br>&emsp;• twist or sprain (an ankle)<br>move (something) so that it is in a different position in relation to its surroundings or its previous position<br>&emsp;• change the position of one's body so that one is facing in a different direction<br>&emsp;• move (something) so as to be aimed or pointed in a particular direction<br>&emsp;• change or cause to change direction<br>change in nature, state, form, or colour; become<br>&emsp;• cause to change in nature, state, form, or colour<br>&emsp;• send or put into a specified place or condition<br>&emsp;• pass the age or time of<br>induce or persuade (someone) to act against their country or associates, especially as a spy or informer<br>make (a profit)<br>n. an act of moving something in a circular direction round an axis or point<br>&emsp;• a bend or curve in a road, path, river, etc<br>&emsp;• one round in a coil of rope or other material<br>a change of direction when moving<br>&emsp;• a development or change in a situation<br>&emsp;• a time when one period of time ends and another begins<br>&emsp;• a place where a road meets or branches off another; a turning<br>an opportunity or obligation to do something that comes successively to each of a number of people<br>&emsp;• a short performance, especially one of a number given by different performers in succession<br>&emsp;• a performer giving a short performance<br>a short walk or ride<br>a shock<br>&emsp;• a brief feeling or experience of illness | **turn** down an offer | v. go round, change direction, bend, aim at, sprain, become, convert, reach (the age of)<br>n. rotation, change of direction, bend, opportunity, act, stroll, shock, service | 0 |
